Three `google_service_account` resources (`client_read`, `client_write`, `client_admin`). Their `.email` attribute serves as both `<role>_client_email` and the OAuth2 `<role>_client_id`.\n82: 2. Three `google_service_account_key` resources (`client_read`, `client_write`, `client_admin`) referencing each service account (`service_account_id = google_service_account.client_<role>.name`). Their `.private_key` attribute (base64-encoded key material generated by the GCP IAM service) serves as the OAuth2 `<role>_client_secret`.\n83: \n84: Write these exact fields under `auth` in `/workspace/manifest.json`:\n85: - `tenant_id`: `google_identity_platform_tenant.<name>.name`\n86: - `resource_server_identifier`: `\"https://api.mediapulse.io\"` (or your API resource server URI)\n87: - `issuer`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/identitytoolkit/v3/relyingparty/<gcp_project_id>\"`\n88: - `jwks_url`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/robot/v1/metadata/jwk/securetoken@system.gserviceaccount.com\"` (pass this URL as `FIREBASE_AUTH_JWKS_URL` / `AUTH_JWKS_URL` to the Cloud Run API)\n89: - `token_endpoint`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/oauth2/v4/token\"`\n90: - `read_client_email` and `read_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_read.email`\n91: - `write_client_email` and `write_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_write.email`\n92: - `admin_client_email` and `admin_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_admin.email`\n93: - `read_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_read.private_key`\n94: - `write_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_write.private_key`\n95: - `admin_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_admin.private_key`\n96: \n97: To obtain a Bearer JWT at runtime, send an HTTP `POST` (`application/x-www-form-urlencoded` or JSON) to `auth.token_endpoint` (`<gcp_endpoint_url>/oauth2/v4/token`) with `grant_type=client_credentials`, `client_id=<role_client_id>`, `client_secret=<role_client_secret>`, and `scope=mediapulse/<role>`.\n98: \n99: \n\n(End of file - total 99 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# Runtime\n\nThis file defines the required runtime behavior, service connections and\nenvironment-specific GCP behavior for MediaPulse Ingest. Treat it as a secret and never emit it in logs or `manifest.json`. |\n\n## API\n\nThe API runs on Cloud Run (v2).\n\nRequired variables:\n- `DATABASE_URL`: `postgresql://<user>:<password>@<host>:5432/<database>?sslmode=disable`\n- `DATASTORE_NAMESPACE`: Cloud Datastore namespace for caching.\n- `FIREBASE_AUTH_JWKS_URL`: URL to fetch Identity Platform JWKS.\n- `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`: Exact `gcp_project_id` value from `config.json`.\n\n## Projector Worker\n\nTriggered by Cloud Pub/Sub Push Subscription.\n\nRequired variables:\n- `FIRESTORE_DATABASE`: Cloud Firestore database identifier.\n- `DATASTORE_NAMESPACE`: Cloud Datastore namespace for caching.\n- `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`: Exact `gcp_project_id` value from `config.json`.\n\n## Outbox Relay Worker\n\nTriggered by Cloud Scheduler (1-minute).\n\nRequired variables:\n- `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string.\n- `PUBSUB_TOPIC`: Exact name of the main Pub/Sub topic.\n- `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`: Exact `gcp_project_id` value from `config.json`.\n\n## Audit Archiver Worker\n\nTriggered by Cloud Scheduler (5-minute).\n\nRequired variables:\n- `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string.\n- `GCS_BUCKET`: Exact name of the GCS audit bucket.\n- `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`: Exact `gcp_project_id` value from `config.json`.\n\n## Authentication & OAuth2 Token Exchange (`manifest.auth`)\n\nProvision an Identity Platform tenant (`google_identity_platform_tenant`) and three dedicated client IAM Service Accounts (`google_service_account`) with corresponding user-managed keys (`google_service_account_key`) for the `read` (`mediapulse/read`), `write` (`mediapulse/write`), and `admin` (`mediapulse/admin`) scopes.\n\n### How to Obtain and Output `client_id` and `client_secret`\nIn Terraform/OpenTofu, create:\n1. Three `google_service_account` resources (`client_read`, `client_write`, `client_admin`). Their `.email` attribute serves as both `<role>_client_email` and the OAuth2 `<role>_client_id`.\n2. Three `google_service_account_key` resources (`client_read`, `client_write`, `client_admin`) referencing each service account (`service_account_id = google_service_account.client_<role>.name`). Their `.private_key` attribute (base64-encoded key material generated by the GCP IAM service) serves as the OAuth2 `<role>_client_secret`.\n\nWrite these exact fields under `auth` in `/workspace/manifest.json`:\n- `tenant_id`: `google_identity_platform_tenant.<name>.name`\n- `resource_server_identifier`: `\"https://api.mediapulse.io\"` (or your API resource server URI)\n- `issuer`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/identitytoolkit/v3/relyingparty/<gcp_project_id>\"`\n- `jwks_url`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/robot/v1/metadata/jwk/securetoken@system.gserviceaccount.com\"` (pass this URL as `FIREBASE_AUTH_JWKS_URL` / `AUTH_JWKS_URL` to the Cloud Run API)\n- `token_endpoint`: `\"<gcp_endpoint_url>/oauth2/v4/token\"`\n- `read_client_email` and `read_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_read.email`\n- `write_client_email` and `write_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_write.email`\n- `admin_client_email` and `admin_client_id`: `google_service_account.client_admin.email`\n- `read_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_read.private_key`\n- `write_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_write.private_key`\n- `admin_client_secret`: `google_service_account_key.client_admin.private_key`\n\nTo obtain a Bearer JWT at runtime, send an HTTP `POST` (`application/x-www-form-urlencoded` or JSON) to `auth.token_endpoint` (`<gcp_endpoint_url>/oauth2/v4/token`) with `grant_type=client_credentials`, `client_id=<role_client_id>`, `client_secret=<role_client_secret>`, and `scope=mediapulse/<role>`.\n\n","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":99,"totalLines":99,"truncated":false}},"title":"workspace/contracts/runtime.md","time":{"start":1790347774687,"end":1790347774798}},"metadata":{"openrouter":{"reasoning_details":[]}},"id":"prt_0d90b0aac001RA9dlA6zf7sNva","sessionID":"ses_f26f54069ffeJJP2pa5qxQIfSW","messageID":"msg_0d90b03a3001zKCeSFfHU2BlGP"}}

`processor`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n- Provision either 4 dedicated `google_logging_project_bucket_config` resources (one per microservice) or a centralized `google_logging_project_bucket_config` accompanied by `google_logging_project_sink` resources covering the 4 microservices.\n- Retention: Configure `retention_days >= 14` (e.g. `30` days) on every `google_logging_project_bucket_config`.\n\n===== memorystore.md =====\n# Cloud Datastore Cache Contract\n\n- Engine (`cache.engine`): `\"cloud-datastore\"`.\n- Namespace (`cache.datastore_namespace`): `<resource_prefix>-cache`.\n- Emulator/Endpoint Host (`cache.emulator_host`): Host/port of the Floci-GCP Datastore endpoint (`gcp:4588`).\n- Purpose: Low-latency read-through cache in front of Cloud Firestore projections (`X-Projection-Cache: HIT` / `MISS`).\n\n===== pubsub.md =====\n# Cloud Pub/Sub & Cloud Tasks Contract\n\n- Main Topic (`messaging.topic_id`, `messaging.topic_name`): (`workers.processor_id`) with a `dead_letter_policy` targeting `messaging.dlq_topic_id` and `max_delivery_attempts = 5`.\n- Dead Letter Topic & Subscription (`messaging.dlq_topic_id`, `messaging.dlq_subscription_id`): `google_pubsub_topic` and pull `google_pubsub_subscription` for dead-letter inspection.\n- Rebuild Queue (`messaging.rebuild_tasks_queue`): `google_cloud_tasks_queue` for rate-limited projection rebuild tasks.\n\n===== scheduler.md =====\n# Cloud Scheduler\n\nCreate two `google_cloud_scheduler_job` resources in the deployment region:\n\n- `schedules.relay_job_name`: Recurring schedule (`* * * * *`) triggering the Outbox Relay Cloud Function (`workers.relay_id`) via HTTP POST with OIDC token (`iam.scheduler_service_account`).\n- `schedules.archiver_job_name`: Recurring schedule (`*/5 * * * *`) triggering the Audit Archiver Cloud Function (`workers.archiver_id`) via HTTP POST with OIDC token false`.\n- Create an ingress subnet (`google_compute_subnetwork`) and a private subnet (`google_compute_subnetwork`) with `private_ip_google_access = true`.\n- Record the exact resource IDs in \"INGRESS_TRAFFIC_INTERNAL_ONLY\"` on worker Cloud Run/Cloud Function v2 services.\n- Must use Serverless VPC Access connector (`vpc_access`) with `egress = \"ALL_TRAFFIC\"` (or
